Fire multiple in parallel for documents with multiple audience segments. Does NOT do structural review (developmental-reviewer / structural-reviewer) or voice comparison (voice-matcher). Returns a structured report with narrative summary and evidence table. Does not interact with the user. - **persona-returning-owner**: Someone coming back after hours or days who asks one question first: what did my agents do while I was away? Reviews a pull request, plan, pitch, design or spec from this perspective and reports where it fails them. Never approves. ([jeduden/parleyinn](https://github.com/jeduden/parleyinn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/persona-returning-owner.md))
- **persona-review**: You are given a target: a pull request, a plan, the pitch, a design or a spec. Read it, and the files it touches, from where you stand. ([jeduden/parleyinn](https://github.com/jeduden/parleyinn/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/persona-review.md))
- **persona-review-orchestrator**: Runs a citizen/user persona panel over a piece of UX, copy, or content — samples real-distribution-grounded Korean personas from a catalog, dispatches each as an independent panelist, and synthesizes their reactions into one report. Use for "how would ordinary users react", user-perspective / usability / copy-tone review, or "/persona-review". A complement to code-reviewer/security-reviewer, NOT a replacement — it judges user experience, never code correctness or security. ([joymin5655/Agent](https://github.com/joymin5655/Agent/blob/HEAD/extras/agents/persona-review-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **persona-reviewer**: Reviews a change, PR, plan or skill against the binding personas in docs/PERSONAS.md — which personas it serves, which acceptance scenarios it closes or breaks, whether guard personas are unchanged, and whether the relevance invariants hold. Use before merging any feature, after a work package from docs/plans/, or to audit an existing feature area for one persona. Reports; does not fix. ([fjaeckel/ninerlog-api](https://github.com/fjaeckel/ninerlog-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/persona-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **persona-reviewer**: Reviews a diff or PR as a specific extracted reviewer persona. Given a path to a persona file (personas/&lt;login&gt;.md) and something to review, it produces review comments in that reviewer's voice and priorities. Use one invocation per persona to run a review panel (the reviewer:review command / Agent Reviewer skill). ([ivankuznetsov/agent-plugins](https://github.com/ivankuznetsov/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-reviewer/agents/persona-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **persona-reviewer**: Read-only dispatch target for team personas in review, analysis, or assessment mode. Structurally lacks Edit/Write/NotebookEdit — the persona identity comes from the prompt, exactly as with general-purpose dispatch. ([MotWakorb/ai-agent-dev-team](https://github.com/MotWakorb/ai-agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **persona-reviewer**: Reviews document drafts as one fixed persona whose head file its task prompt names first, during the persona-review skill's panel. Invoke explicitly from that skill; not for general use. ([TaiSakuma/reviewed-writer](https://github.com/TaiSakuma/reviewed-writer/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **persona-reviewer**: One fixed-viewpoint reviewer used inside the revision loop (skim reader, close reader, positioning expert, numbers auditor, hostile reviewer). Returns complaints as structured data with quotes. Never sees earlier reviews or the change log. ([debsagar/paperloop](https://github.com/debsagar/paperloop/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-reviewer.md))

- **persona-reviewer**: A reviewer deciding whether a room's branch may land: reads the story, the diff and the evidence, and signs off or asks for changes. - **phase-a-fixer**: Phase A subagent: fix review findings, push code, write handoff file, print exit report. Used after a PR receives CR/BugBot/Greptile review findings. ([auerbachb/claude-code-config](https://github.com/auerbachb/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-a-fixer.md) · ★5)
- **phase-acceptance**: Phase/milestone completion validator. Use before opening a PR that closes out a build-guide phase or milestone to verify all PRD requirements and verification checkpoints are met. Reads the relevant PRD section and build guide phase, then checks the code against every requirement. ([jacarty/coding-toolkit](https://github.com/jacarty/coding-toolkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-acceptance.md) · 2 copies)
- **phase-acceptance-validator**: Validates a phase-acceptance PR (label "phase-acceptance") against the phase's acceptance issue checklist. Verifies tier-gated coverage, OpenAPI snapshot freshness, design-discovery doc linked (frontend phases), no TODO/HACK in production paths, and all phase deliverables present. Use when a PR carries the phase-acceptance label. ([goldr0g3r/lotusgift_old](https://github.com/goldr0g3r/lotusgift_old/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/phase-acceptance-validator.md))
- **phase-adversarial-auditor**: Whole-phase adversarial code audit of a CAOS repair phase on Fable 5.1 at high reasoning, run after the phase confidence review and its remediation. Never per task. ([EricMG13/caos-v2](https://github.com/EricMG13/caos-v2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-adversarial-auditor.md))
- **phase-adversary**: You are an independent, skeptical reviewer of ONE analysis phase's output — recon, architecture, threat-model, or C1 context. Your job is to find where that analysis is **inaccurate, overreaching, or missing an obvious area**, before any later phase trusts it. You reason statically, READ-ONLY; you never build or run the target. ([cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools](https://github.com/cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/sec-overlay/skills/sec-overlay/agents/phase-adversary.md) · 2 copies)
- **phase-agent**: Use this agent when a phase of a zforge feature plan is ready to implement and its work should run in its own context. Typical triggers include /zforge:feature-orchestrate spawning a READY phase, resuming a phase whose agent was interrupted mid-run, and re-running a phase after a decision was rejected in review. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. Do not use it for work with no phase file — it reads its instructions from one. ([Langerrr/zforge](https://github.com/Langerrr/zforge/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-agent.md) · ★12)
- **phase-agent**: Executes a single phase using only the validated phase bundle and route-backed validation. ([emanuel74-bit/Autonomous-Agent-Activator-](https://github.com/emanuel74-bit/Autonomous-Agent-Activator-/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-agent.md))
- **phase-approval-agent**: Phase approval gate that verifies scope coverage, required checks, and guardrail compliance before proceeding. ([bigeasyfreeman/agent-framework](https://github.com/bigeasyfreeman/agent-framework/blob/HEAD/templates/claude/.claude/agents/phase-approval-agent.md) · ★18)
- **phase-approver**: Phase gate agent for the FeatureWorkflow-orchestrator. Decides on its own whether a finished Design or Implement phase may be merged into the main feature branch, by verifying the phase's artifacts, review reports, decisions ledgers, build and tests against the issue and the repository rules. Returns APPROVE, REJECT (with the step to redo) or ESCALATE (asks the human). Use when: approve phase, phase gate, auto-approve design phase, auto-approve implement phase. ([onCodeDesign/Infra-Demo-AI](https://github.com/onCodeDesign/Infra-Demo-AI/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/phase-approver.agent.md))
- **phase-architect**: Master orchestrator for phase execution. Analyzes roadmap, creates execution plans, coordinates parallel development. Use PROACTIVELY for any phase execution. ([ViperJuice/claude-code-template](https://github.com/ViperJuice/claude-code-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-architect.md) · ★4)
- **phase-assessor**: Phase-level test assessor. Creates integration test specifications, data format contracts, and API shapes BEFORE task testers run, so all tasks within a phase produce compatible interfaces. ([RhizomaticRobin/Gatekeeper](https://github.com/RhizomaticRobin/Gatekeeper/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-assessor.md))
- **phase-attempt-agent**: 정확히 한 번의 phase 시도를 fresh context에서 실행하고 요약 결과만 반환하는 fork agent. ([munlucky/moonshot-relay](https://github.com/munlucky/moonshot-relay/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-attempt-agent.ko.md))
- **phase-attempt-agent**: Fork-based agent that executes exactly one isolated phase attempt and returns a summarized result. ([munlucky/moonshot-relay](https://github.com/munlucky/moonshot-relay/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-attempt-agent.md))
- **phase-auditor**: Auditor adversarial de fases do SDLC. Sua missao e ENCONTRAR PROBLEMAS, nao validar. Assume mindset de desafio: "ha problemas, preciso acha-los". Use este agente para: - Auditoria pos-gate de qualquer fase - Analise critica adversarial - Classificacao de problemas (CRITICO a LEVE) - Decisao: aprovar, reprovar, ou corrigir Examples: - &lt;example&gt; Context: Phase 5 passou pelo gate orchestrator: "Audite Phase 5 - Implementation" assistant: "Vou usar @phase-auditor para analise adversarial" &lt;commentary&gt; Auditor busca ativamente problemas que self-validation pode ter perdido &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([arbgjr/sdlc_agentico](https://github.com/arbgjr/sdlc_agentico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-auditor.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)

([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-checker.md))
- **phase-close-evaluator**: Fresh-context skeptical reviewer for a claimed-complete phase. Has no Write/Edit tools and no memory of the build. Reads the diff, the claimed evidence, and this project's own phase-close checklist, then returns PASS or NEEDS_WORK with specific findings. Sits alongside (not replacing) the Qwen skeptic pass -- use this for judgement-heavy phase closes and Expert-Hour simulation, Qwen for volume/cheap passes. ([21bcarlisle-arch/synthetic-enterprise](https://github.com/21bcarlisle-arch/synthetic-enterprise/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-close-evaluator.md) · ★2)

Blocks phases from completing when UI artifacts are missing or the feature is backend-only but described as complete product capability. Final gate before finalize. ([dennisccy/incredible_auto_dev](https://github.com/dennisccy/incredible_auto_dev/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-closure-auditor.md))
- **phase-code-reviewer**: Strictly read-only reviewer for a complete phase implementation. Reviews the full diff of the current phase branch (committed, staged, unstaged and untracked changes) against the base branch and returns severity-classified findings with a single APPROVED / CHANGES_REQUIRED decision. Never edits files. Use during the /phase-loop workflow after the quality gate passes. ([Tamchow123/Safwa](https://github.com/Tamchow123/Safwa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-code-reviewer.md))
- **phase-code-reviewer**: Phase workflow only (skills/phase/process-phase). Optional focused code review when requested or needed for a concrete correctness concern. Inspects the relevant diff and reuses current test results; reproduces uncertainty with focused tests only. Never starts full-suite, replay or evidence-review loops, and never edits source or status. Not the milestone workflow's independent review, which runs in a different harness. ([vasiloglou/dev-tools](https://github.com/vasiloglou/dev-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/phase-code-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)

- **phase-coordinator**: Phase + doc lockstep coordinator. MUST be invoked (no confirmation) at three moments: Mode A BEFORE the first non-trivial edit on a new `claude/*` branch (scope-collision scan vs active branches + recent PRs); Mode B BEFORE opening a PR / flipping Draft → Ready (CLAUDE.md + AGENTS.md lockstep per §Conventions); Mode C AFTER a phase / sub-PR completes (PHASE_STATUS.md + SKILL.md + WORKFLOW.md triple-doc bump). Wraps branch-collision-check + claude-md-lockstep-check + phase-status-bump. Read + Bash; proposes doc edits for user authorization. - **phase-coordinator**: Owns one phase or debt-drain window end-to-end by DELEGATING — dispatches reader-digester/gsd-executor/gsd-code-reviewer/gsd-verifier sub-agents, never does leaf work itself. Spawn as a milestone-scoped C2 (dispatched by the top level) or a per-phase C1 (dispatched by a C2) — tier = charter scope, ORCHESTRATION §3/§11 — for work that is a wave of sub-lanes (fable top-level inherits; a no-fable top-level passes an explicit model override, e.g. `model: opus`). - **phase-implementer**: Retired implementation delegate retained for compatibility. The supervisor uses the coder tooling in process-phase/references/implement.md. Current policy is focused TDD and one final full regression run; independent reviews and replay/evidence-review loops are not mandatory. ([vasiloglou/dev-tools](https://github.com/vasiloglou/dev-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/dvt-phase/agents/phase-implementer.md) · 2 copies)
- **phase-implementer-max**: Escalation variant of phase-implementer (Opus, maximum reasoning effort) for the skill-evals program's hard surfaces — Phase 2's harness/safety build, Phase 8's driver spike — or relaunching a phase where the standard implementer struggled. Same contract as phase-implementer. ([Intentional-Society/is-app](https://github.com/Intentional-Society/is-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-implementer-max.md) · ★1)
- **phase-implementor**: Implements one phase of an approved plan under an orchestrator's direction. Spawned by the orchestrate-implementation-claude skill. Does file-level work, runs every verify check, and stops rather than guessing on ambiguity. ([AmirinNS/coding-agent-skills](https://github.com/AmirinNS/coding-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/orchestrate-implementation-claude/agents/phase-implementor.md))
- **phase-iv-orchestrator**: Use this agent when executing Phase IV of the Todo-app project following spec-driven development workflow. This agent should be invoked when:\n\n1. The user explicitly requests Phase IV execution or advancement\n2. Phase III has been completed and validated\n3. A systematic, coordinated approach to Phase IV tasks is required\n4. Multiple specialized agents need to be orchestrated in sequence\n5. Tracking of Phase IV artifacts and decisions is needed\n\nExamples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nuser: "We've completed Phase III. Let's start Phase IV."\nassistant: "I'm going to use the Task tool to launch the pha… ([TayyabAziz11/todo-app-hackathon](https://github.com/TayyabAziz11/todo-app-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-iv-orchestrator.md))
- **phase-judge**: Read-only judge of a whole OneRM phase - checks every card of the phase together against the specification, CLAUDE.md and the conventions, finds what per-PR reviews miss, and for each specification gap or decision gives options with a recommendation. Launched by the card skill when a phase closes. ([ivomiyashiro/onerm](https://github.com/ivomiyashiro/onerm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-judge.md))
- **phase-lead**: Opus phase lead. Owns one phase of a plan end to end - re-plans it, does the thinking and the smart work, hands mundane tasks to worker (Sonnet) and lookups to drone (Haiku), starts any other agent the repo provides or another phase lead for a sub-phase, verifies, and reports. Use when the orchestrator dispatches a phase of a larger job. ([theGobindSingh/cleantank](https://github.com/theGobindSingh/cleantank/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/gobind/agents/phase-lead.md))
- **phase-learning-fetcher**: AI-200 의 특정 session 에 해당하는 MS Learn 학습 경로의 모든 모듈/단원을 WebFetch 로 정독해 단원 표 + 핵심 결정 포인트 + 본 레포 적용 메모를 정리해서 돌려준다. CLAUDE.md §2 (학습 경로 정독 의무화) 의 자동 강제 + main agent 컨텍스트 절약. session 작업 진입 직전 /phase-start 슬래시 커맨드가 호출. ([ThisTimeNull/azure-ai-200-certification-challenge](https://github.com/ThisTimeNull/azure-ai-200-certification-challenge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase-learning-fetcher.md) · ★3)
